Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the surfaces structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 764. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 653 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the surfaces decomposition. By minimality, the element a^764 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 653-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
